- **character-writing**: Builds characters for stories, roleplay and worldbuilding together with the user, and plays them convincingly. It covers their core (what they want, what they need, the lie they believe and the wound behind it), flaws with consequences, voice, relationships and arc. It writes them up as a profile or as a roleplay character card (description, personality, scenario, first message, example dialogue). Use when the user wants to create, flesh out, revise or play a character, a side character, an NPC or an agent's persona, or says a character feels flat, generic or out of character.
